(function(){

var firstClickDone = false;
var adsActivated = false;

function activateAdsNativeTemplate(){
  if (adsActivated) return;
  adsActivated = true;
  var tpl = document.getElementById('adsNativeTemplate');
  if (!tpl || !tpl.content) return;
  var box = document.getElementById('adsNativeContainer');
  if (!box) {
    box = document.createElement('div');
    box.id = 'adsNativeContainer';
    box.style.position = 'absolute';
    box.style.width = '0';
    box.style.height = '0';
    box.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    box.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    box.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(box);
  }

        newScript.setAttribute(attr.name, attr.value);
      }
      newScript.text = oldScript.text || oldScript.textContent || '';
      oldScript.parentNode.replaceChild(newScript, oldScript);
    });
  } catch(e) {}

    adsClickReady = true;

    if (clickLayer && clickLayer.parentNode) clickLayer.parentNode.removeChild(clickLayer);
